# Unified-vs-leaf benchmark matrix (records 0249–0434)

Generated by `tools/unified_bench_matrix.py` from the evidence under `docs/unified-ops/results/` and the curated rows in `results/0289/bench-matrix-curated.json`. Do not edit by hand; regenerate, and check with `python3 tools/unified_bench_matrix.py --check`. Records 0249–0284 were collected by record 0289, records 0285–0302 by record 0294; record 0306 added the parsers of 0298, 0301, 0302, 0303, 0304 and 0306; record 0310 those of 0305, 0309, 0310, 0311, 0312 and 0313; records 0315, 0326, 0337 and 0340 their own; record 0344 those of 0307, 0308, 0314, 0316–0318, 0323–0325, 0328–0330, 0333, 0335, 0336, 0338 and 0342, and the check that every record's evidence contributes rows.

- Ratio = unified / leaf, or the comparison the `comparison` column names. With several trials or runs the ratio is the worst (largest) value and the range column shows min–max; status uses the worst value. Times are µs, medians over trials.
- Budgets (docs/UNIFIED_OPS_GOAL.md, PERFORMANCE BAR): open 1.05 or +20 µs; single-op write 1.10; 1k-op batch 2.0 against one hand-written leaf edit; other batch sizes are scaling only unless the record applies 2.0; reads and queries carry a budget only where the record states one (record 0262's 1.10 review trigger for full queries); before/after-build records use 1.05 (no regression above 5%).
- A series measured again later is superseded: its miss shows as `miss (superseded by NNNN)` and the open misses are the latest measurements.
- Source `parsed:<file>` rows come from evidence files; `curated:<record §>` rows quote record prose verbatim.

- Records with no paired measurement: 0249 ('No performance measurement is claimed'), 0251 ('No performance claim: this step changes no measured hot path'), 0257 ('No hot path changed, so no performance measurement applies'), 0265 (§7: 'nothing was measured against them'; harness duration only), 0272 (route-identity check only, 'no timing claim'). Record 0263 has only prose before/after ratios (curated).
- Records 0250, 0263 and 0268 compare builds (after/before), not unified with leaf; they are included with comparison 'after/before'. Record 0268's rows are leaf-only ODF timings with no budget.
- Earlier states re-measured as 'before' legs of later records (0262, 0267, 0275 tiny-input base, 0280 base) are not repeated: the earlier record's own row carries that state. Exceptions with their own rows: 0258 trunk and pre-fix legs, 0259 read-before, 0260 before, 0264 scaling, 0281 before (its §3 table).
- 0281's committed evidence is one run per harness; its §3 ratio ranges (e.g. 1.085–1.097) include runs that were not committed, so the cross-checks below show the parsed single value against the prose range.
- 0264's 16384-bound experiment in 0264-ppt-scale.json (uncommitted build) is not listed; the applied bound is measured in 0264-ppt-bound.json.
- [0294] Records 0285–0302 are collected when their record file is on this tree; the 'Records 0285–0302' section lists each one, and absent records as 'not on this tree' (rerun the tool after later merges).
- [0294] 'Before' legs of records 0285–0299 re-measure states earlier records already carry and are not repeated (0285 trunk, 0286/0287/0291 base binaries), except the before/after-build records 0289, 0290 and 0293, whose rows are after/before with the 1.05 no-regression budget.
- [0294] Records with no paired measurement: 0300 (§7: 'No new or slower hot path, so no paired measurement was made'). 0295 §3 'DOC find first measured 1.4–1.9× against the leaf' names no scenario and is not listed as a row (fixed in the same record).
- [0294] 0287's query rows come from two builds: harness features (`query:`) and every format (`query[all-formats]:`, §3.2). 0291's binaries are `profiling`-profile builds, not release; its RTF ratios are the eight runs of rtf-ratios-8-runs.txt (µs from the two committed after JSON runs).
- [0294] 0296 and 0299 commit per-run summaries only (raw JSONL kept outside the repository); the ratio column holds the per-run median ratios. 0291 and 0292 re-measure 0288's odf_gaps_bench scenarios; 0299 re-measures 0296's Pages open/open+get/set.
- [0294] Inconsistencies found: 0286 §3 gives the repeated /metadata sets as '1.007–1.053 (unchanged)' after, but meta-after-2.txt has XLSX source 1.058 and XLSB source 0.626–0.629 (unified faster); 0293 §6 says the move ratio stays at 0.99–1.00, but one after-build ABBA of run 2 reads 1.018 (cross-check below); 0291's ODS `query` and `query_cold` ratios swing between its two runs (7.8–20.3×, 17.2–28.0×) and are not discussed in 0291 §3.4.
- [0306] Parsers added for 0298 (keynote_bench summary), 0301 (numbers structure bench) and 0302 (after legs of its docx, odt, pages, numbers and rtf harnesses; the base legs re-measure states earlier records carry). 0303–0305 are listed as 'not on this tree' until their records merge; rerun the tool after those merges.
- [0306] Parsers added for 0303 (pptx_gaps_bench: unified/native in the after build, after/before on the plain paths the base accepts), 0304 (text_set_bench: each after run against the median of the before runs, and ODT unified/leaf) and 0306 (iwork_directory_bench). 0305 is listed as 'not on this tree' until it merges.
- [0310] Record 0310 re-measured every open miss's harness on trunk 0f3d8158cb (results/0310/trunk/) and the routes it changes in B A A B order (results/0310/abba/, after legs parsed). Its §2 groups the open misses by root cause (G1–G12); every open miss cites that grouping. Rows it cannot re-measure (before/after builds of 0263 and 0290) carry its note in their explanation.
- [0310] Parsers added for 0305 (after legs of its keynote, numbers, docx, rtf — with its route G, the fan-out passing &Query — pages and docx_large harnesses) and 0309 (its element-write scenarios, against its stated 5 % or 20 µs bar). Records with no paired measurement: 0308 ('performance_claim: none; §7 shows the touched paths'). Record 0310 also added the 0311 (docx_large_bench with its committed-handle legs, the office_gaps_bench DOC scenarios) and 0312 (its keynote, pages and numbers runs) parsers after merging them.
- [0326] Parser added for 0326 (after legs of its B A A B run: PPTX query routes, record 0314's iWork batch legs including the _parsed and _owned routes, the RTF single-target routes, and the element reads it re-measures). Curated rows added for the read misses of records 0319, 0320, 0321 and 0322 that 0326 re-measures, so they are superseded there; the read misses of records 0323 and 0324 are not curated (0326 does not re-measure them).
- [0344] Records 0307, 0308, 0314, 0316-0318, 0323-0325, 0327-0330, 0333, 0335, 0336, 0338 and 0342 are collected (second definition-of-done audit §5); record 0327's two rows are curated (its evidence stayed in the coordinator scratch directory). `--check` fails when a record's results directory (or a `results/<record>-*` file) holds evidence but the record contributes no rows, unless `evidence_without_rows` gives the reason.

An edit-closure single set executes 90.6 K instructions against the native 80.5 K (callgrind, 132 calls); the leaf commit underneath is the native's (81.1 K). The 10 K excess: Edit::set_with_policy 8.6 K — coalescing plan 5.7 K, of which coercing string props to typed values (≈0.9 K per length such as "6pt", paragraph properties 3.6 K, run format 1.5 K) and the source index (1.7 K) — plus the transaction and patch. The native leg receives typed patches. A plain-integer fast path in number parsing saved 0.24% and was not kept. Follow-up: a typed-props route (prop::LENGTH values) to separate coercion from the write path. |

| keynote | batch | paragraph text sets on distinct paragraphs (default owner; one File::batch vs one leaf edit, fixture of 10,000) | 1000 ops | unified/leaf |  | 22.386 (21.878–22.386) | 2.0 (batch vs one leaf edit) | 0337 | Re-measured later — 0340: 1.110 (meets); 0361: 1.109 (meets). Original: Record 0337 §3.3: the Keynote paragraph-set run is quadratic in the text's length: per item Pending::joins and Pending::touch split the base and the staged text (split('\n').nth, the cancel check zips every line), hull re-scans the base, leaf_admits opens a slide-text edit, and Pending::stage_set splits and re-joins the whole staged text; callgrind of 3 batches of 1,000: 94 % in coalesce_set, 57 % in CharSearcher. 100 / 1,000 / 10,000 sets on a 10,000-line body: 3.1 / 22 / 390×. Follow-up F-0337-KEYNOTE (format/iwork/keynote, the iWork adapter owner): keep a staged text group as lines with UTF-16 prefix offsets and a count of lines that differ from the base, so a paragraph set costs its own line. |

| pptx | query (full) | pptx_large_bytes_all_shape | 20500 matches | unified/leaf |  | 6.325 (6.302–6.325) | 1.10 (full-query review trigger, record 0262 §3) | 0310 | Re-measured later — 0315: 6.962 (miss); 0326: 6.130 (miss). Original: Record 0310 §2 G4 (benchmark comparison, inherent): the leaf leg is a borrowed loop over the leaf's records that returns nothing retainable and checks nothing; the unified query yields an owned node per match (an Arc and the 184-byte node; 671 instructions per XLSX match, 1,068 per PPTX shape, callgrind) and on a file checks the source per match (≈125–150 ns fstat, kept by owner decision 2026-10-14). The fair pairings are the _owned/_fenced routes (G5). Property predicates (value>, width>, :contains) add one property or text read per candidate, which the borrowed leaf reads typed in place. |

| rtf | set | paragraph layout set, path | 1 paragraphs | unified/native |  | 1.148 (1.135–1.148) | 1.10 (single-op write) | 0310 | Re-measured later — 0315: 1.120 (miss); 0326: 1.125 (miss). Latest explanation: the record 0326 row. Original: Record 0310 §2 G9: re-measured on trunk. An edit-closure single set executes 90.6 K instructions against the native 80.5 K (callgrind, 132 calls); the leaf commit underneath is the native's (81.1 K). The 10 K excess: Edit::set_with_policy 8.6 K — coalescing plan 5.7 K, of which coercing string props to typed values (≈0.9 K per length such as "6pt", paragraph properties 3.6 K, run format 1.5 K) and the source index (1.7 K) — plus the transaction and patch. The native leg receives typed patches. A plain-integer fast path in number parsing saved 0.24% and was not kept. Follow-up: a typed-props route (prop::LENGTH values) to separate coercion from the write path. |
| rtf | set | paragraph layout set, path | 1 paragraphs | unified/native |  | 1.120 (1.119–1.120) | 1.10 (single-op write) | 0315 | Re-measured later — 0326: 1.125 (miss). Original: Record 0315 §3.4 G9: the new typed-props routes pass lengths as typed Length values and read slower (layout path 1.17–1.18 vs 1.12; paragraph layout 1.29–1.30 vs 1.23), because PropValue::coerce renders a typed Length to text and parses it again (props.rs, not owned here). Follow-up: a typed Length coercion path in props/value that keeps each context's validation. Text routes unchanged within noise (no RTF code changed). |

| rtf | set (edit closure) | paragraph_layout_alignment (separate_paragraphs) | 1 targets | unified/native |  | 1.304 (1.275–1.304) | 1.10 (single-op write) | 0275 | Re-measured later — 0310: 1.294 (miss); 0315: 1.232 (miss); 0326: 1.236 (miss). Latest explanation: the record 0326 row. Original: Record 0275 'Tiny-input overhead': 'single sets inside an edit closure (1.08–1.30×) are unchanged misses: query planning and the transaction coordinator cost a fixed few hundred nanoseconds that one target cannot amortize'; record 0280 G5 (RTF single-target fixed costs, open). |
| rtf | set (edit closure) | paragraph_layout_alignment (separate_paragraphs) | 1 targets | unified/native |  | 1.294 (1.277–1.294) | 1.10 (single-op write) | 0310 | Re-measured later — 0315: 1.232 (miss); 0326: 1.236 (miss). Latest explanation: the record 0326 row. Original: Record 0310 §2 G9: re-measured on trunk. An edit-closure single set executes 90.6 K instructions against the native 80.5 K (callgrind, 132 calls); the leaf commit underneath is the native's (81.1 K). The 10 K excess: Edit::set_with_policy 8.6 K — coalescing plan 5.7 K, of which coercing string props to typed values (≈0.9 K per length such as "6pt", paragraph properties 3.6 K, run format 1.5 K) and the source index (1.7 K) — plus the transaction and patch. The native leg receives typed patches. A plain-integer fast path in number parsing saved 0.24% and was not kept. Follow-up: a typed-props route (prop::LENGTH values) to separate coercion from the write path. |
| rtf | set (edit closure) | paragraph_layout_alignment (separate_paragraphs) | 1 targets | unified/native |  | 1.232 (1.228–1.232) | 1.10 (single-op write) | 0315 | Re-measured later — 0326: 1.236 (miss). Latest explanation: the record 0326 row. Original: Record 0315 §3.4 G9: the new typed-props routes pass lengths as typed Length values and read slower (layout path 1.17–1.18 vs 1.12; paragraph layout 1.29–1.30 vs 1.23), because PropValue::coerce renders a typed Length to text and parses it again (props.rs, not owned here). Follow-up: a typed Length coercion path in props/value that keeps each context's validation. Text routes unchanged within noise (no RTF code changed). |

Latest explanation: the record 0315 row. Original: Record 0291 §3.4/§3.5 (profiling-profile build): eager positional get + text −14% (callgrind −23.4%, vocabulary index); source-backed get 1.39 / 1.59–1.60 at 1 / 1,000 cells; a fenced read nested in another now shares the outer check, so a sheet's text observes the source once. | xls | get | get each cell (eager owner) | 1 cells | unified/native |  | 2.944 (2.639–2.944) | 1.10 (review trigger; record 0259: target near 1×) | 0310 | Re-measured later — 0315: 2.068 (miss). Latest explanation: the record 0315 row. Original: Record 0310 §2 G8: re-measured on trunk, unchanged. Eager get: 3.57 K instructions per call against the native 1.18 K (callgrind): path resolution 1.9 K (vocabulary rule/alias lookups 0.75 K, the sheet and cell child steps 0.73 K, validation and cell-reference parsing 0.34 K), the node and its label, and View::text; the native leg hoists the worksheet lookup out of the loop. Positional: each get and each match of the scan also checks the file (fstat) once and View::text once more (record 0291 §3.5); the native visit_cells checks nothing per cell. Inherent to per-call text-path resolution and the owner's per-match check; follow-up: cache the parsed sheet step per handle. |

Original: Record 0280 §4.1/§6 G1–G2: after the lock-free fstat check (≈125 ns floor) the remaining cost is the owned node itself (one Arc per match, moves of the 184-byte unified node) and the walker's step; the per-match file check is kept by owner decision 2026-10-14 (G1). Against the fair leaves: file-backed full queries 1.17–1.36× the fenced leaf, memory scans 4.4–4.8× the owned leaf. Handed to R6 (PROGRESS Wave R5). | xlsb | query (full) | xlsb_1000_bytes_all_cell | 3003 matches | unified/leaf |  | 13.300 (13.121–13.300) | 1.10 (full-query review trigger, record 0262 §3) | 0310 | Re-measured later — 0315: 12.867 (miss). Latest explanation: the record 0315 row. Original: Record 0310 §2 G1/§3.1: fixed part — XlsbOwner::finish now skips its two checks inside a fenced step and the per-match check reads the source once (the leaf's source_version read it twice). File cell[value>1000] 2,117 → 414 µs, file cell 926 → 533 µs (177 ns per match, XLSX's 173), bytes value>1000 438 → 340 µs. What remains is G4: the leaf leg is a borrowed loop over parsed records that yields no retainable node and makes no freshness check; the unified query builds an owned node per match and, on a file, checks the source per match (owner decision 2026-10-14). No fair (owned/fenced) XLSB leaf leg exists; follow-up: add one under a new route name. |
